function updChallengeDisplay(arg3) -- line: 48
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] v4 (copy)
    --  [2] ChallengeList (copy)
    --  [3] LocalPlayer (copy)

    if v4 then
        return
    end

    script.Parent.Visible = true

    for _, val in next, ChallengeList:GetChildren() do
        if val.ClassName == "Frame" then
            val:Destroy()
        end
    end

    local v16 = "master"

    for _, val_1 in next, arg3.standard do
        if val_1.completed ~= true then
            v16 = "standard"
            break
        end
    end

    local t = {}

    for key_2, val_2 in next, arg3[v16] do
        local v17 = script.ChallengeFrame:Clone()

        if val_2.allOrNothing == true or val_2.amtRequired == 1 then
            v17.Amount.Visible = false
            v17.Title.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 1, 0)
        end

        if v16 == "standard" then
            v17.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(52, 152, 219)
        elseif v16 == "master" then
            v17.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(241, 196, 15)
        end

        v17.Title.Text = val_2.desc

        if val_2.amtRequired > 100 then
            v17.Amount.Text = math.floor(val_2.amtCurrent / val_2.amtRequired * 100) .. "%"
        else
            v17.Amount.Text = val_2.amtCurrent .. "/" .. val_2.amtRequired
        end

        v17.ProgressBar.Percent.Size = UDim2.new(val_2.amtCurrent / val_2.amtRequired, 0, 1, 0)
        v17.PremiumOnly.Visible = if val_2.isPremium ~= true then false else LocalPlayer.MembershipType ~= Enum.MembershipType.Premium
        v17.LayoutOrder = key_2
        v17.Visible = val_2.amtCurrent < val_2.amtRequired and true
        v17.Parent = ChallengeList

        if val_2.isPremium == true then
            if (LocalPlayer.MembershipType == Enum.MembershipType.Premium or val_2.isPremium ~= true) and val_2.amtCurrent < val_2.amtRequired then
                table.insert(t, val_2)
            end
        elseif val_2.isPremium ~= true and val_2.amtCurrent < val_2.amtRequired then
            table.insert(t, val_2)
        end
    end

    ChallengeList.MasterPromotion.Visible = #t > 0
end

Remote.UpdPlayerData.OnClientEvent:connect(function(arg4, arg5) -- line: 116

    --  [1] DailyChallenges (ref)

    DailyChallenges = arg4.dailyChallenges
end)

    until tick() - 1 > g_tick

    ChallengeList.CycleChallenges.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(241, 219, 128)
end)

function repeatFind(arg3, arg4, arg5, arg6) -- line: 71

        until v25
    end

    return v25
end

function curWaitCam() -- line: 775
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] QS_m (copy)
    --  [2] v57 (copy)
    --  [3] v58 (ref)
    --  [4] vec3 (ref)
    --  [5] v14 (ref)

    local now_4 = tick()
    local v167 = now_4 * 2
    local v168 = QS_m.s(now_4 * 2)
    local New_2 = CFrame.new
    local New_3 = Vector3.new
    local v175 = v57[v58] * CFrame.Angles(
        QS_m.s(v167 * 1.666) * 0.01,
        v168 * 0.04,
        QS_m.s(v167 * 0.1) * 0.01
    ) * New_2(
        v168,
        math.noise(v168, QS_m.s(now_4 * 2.222), QS_m.s(now_4 * 1.666) + QS_m.s(v167 * 1.666) * 0.02),
        0
    ).p

    return New_2(v175, vec3)
end

function setMapToLowDetail(arg29, arg30) -- line: 810
    local LocalEventScript = arg29:FindFirstChild("LocalEventScript")

    for _, val_17 in next, arg30 do
        if val_17 and val_17.Parent then
            local v180 = false
            local v181 = val_17:IsA("BasePart")

            if v181 then
                v181 = val_17.Name:find("_Water")
            end

            if not v181 then
                if val_17.Name == "MapUI" and val_17.ClassName == "ScreenGui" and val_17.Parent == arg29.Settings then
                    v180 = true
                end

                if table.find(game:GetService("CollectionService"):GetTags(val_17), "FE2_Detail") then
                    v180 = true
                end

                local Parent = val_17.Parent
                local v182 = Parent:IsA("BasePart")

                if v182 then
                    v182 = Parent.Name:find("_Water")
                end

                if not v182 then
                    if (val_17.ClassName == "Texture" or val_17.ClassName == "Decal") and val_17.Parent:IsA(
                        "BasePart"
                    ) and val_17.Parent.Transparency < 0.9 then
                        val_17.Transparency = if not LocalEventScript then val_17.Transparency else 1
                        v180 = not LocalEventScript
                    end

                    if val_17.ClassName == "SurfaceGui" then
                        if LocalEventScript then
                            val_17.Enabled = false
                        else
                            v180 = true
                        end
                    end

                    if val_17.ClassName == "Beam" and val_17.Parent.Name ~= "SlideBeam" and not val_17.Parent:FindFirstChild(
                        "_Wall"
                    ) then
                        if LocalEventScript then
                            val_17.Enabled = false
                        else
                            val_17:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                end

                if table.find(game:GetService("CollectionService"):GetTags(val_17), "FE2_Detail") then
                    if val_17.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                        if val_17.Parent.Transparency < 0.9 then
                            v180 = true
                        end
                    else
                        v180 = true
                    end
                end

                if val_17.ClassName == "ParticleEmitter" or val_17.ClassName == "Smoke" or val_17.ClassName == "Fire" or val_17.ClassName == "Sparkles" or val_17.ClassName == "Trail" then
                    if LocalEventScript then
                        val_17.Enabled = false
                    else
                        v180 = true
                    end
                end

                if val_17.ClassName == "Sky" then
                    val_17.CelestialBodiesShown = false
                end
            end

            if v180 == true then
                val_17:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

    print("Set", arg29.Name, "to Low Detail")
end

function updMapEventInfo(arg63, arg64, arg65) -- line: 2097
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] v30 (copy)
    --  [2] QS_m (copy)

    if arg63 then
        v30.MapEventInfo.Text = arg63
        v30.MapEventInfo.TextColor3 = v379
        v30.MapEventInfo.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.625
        v30.MapEventInfo.TextTransparency = 0
        v30.MapEventInfo:TweenSize(
            UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.8, 0),
            "Out",
            "Quint",
            0.5,
            true
        )
        local v380 = tick() + arg65
        local _ = { v379, Color3.new(1, 0, 0) }

        while true do
            if not (v380 > tick()) then
                break
            end

            v30.MapEventInfo.TextColor3 = v379:lerp(
                Color3.new(1, 0, 0),
                (math.abs((QS_m.s(tick() * 3))))
            )
            wait()
        end

        v30.MapEventInfo.TextColor3 = v379
        v30.MapEventInfo.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
        v30.MapEventInfo.TextTransparency = 0

        if v30.MapEventInfo.Size.Y.Scale >= 0.79 then
            v30.MapEventInfo:TweenSize(
                UDim2.new(1.2, 0, 0.35, 0),
                "Out",
                "Quint",
                0.3,
                true
            )

            return
        end
    else
        v30.MapEventInfo.Text = ""
    end
end

function toggleParticle(arg97, arg98) -- line: 2868
    if arg97.ClassName == "ParticleEmitter" or arg97.ClassName == "BillboardGui" or arg97.ClassName == "Sparkles" or arg97.ClassName == "Fire" or arg97.ClassName == "Smoke" or arg97.ClassName == "Trail" or arg97.ClassName == "Beam" then
        for _, val_38 in next, { "Bubbles", "PlrInfo", "SlideParticles" } do
            if arg97.Name == val_38 then
                return
            end
        end

        if not arg97:FindFirstChild("_IgnoreAura") then
            arg97.Enabled = arg98
        end
    end
end

function updAuraVisible(arg99) -- line: 2882
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] v26 (ref)
    --  [2] LocalPlayer (copy)

    arg99.DescendantAdded:connect(function(descendant_1) -- line: 2884
        -- upvalues:
        --  [1] v26 (ref)
        --  [2] arg99 (copy)
        --  [3] LocalPlayer (ref)

        if descendant_1.Name == "PlayBeam" then
            return
        end

        if descendant_1.ClassName == "ParticleEmitter" or descendant_1.ClassName == "BillboardGui" or descendant_1.ClassName == "Sparkles" or descendant_1.ClassName == "Fire" or descendant_1.ClassName == "Smoke" or descendant_1.ClassName == "Trail" or descendant_1.ClassName == "Beam" then
            toggleParticle(
                descendant_1,
                v26.options.Main.AurasEnabled.Property.Value <= 2 and arg99 == LocalPlayer.Character or v26.options.Main.AurasEnabled.Property.Value == 1
            )
        end
    end)

    for _, val_39 in next, arg99:GetDescendants() do
        if val_39.Name ~= "PlayBeam" and (val_39.ClassName == "ParticleEmitter" or (val_39.ClassName == "BillboardGui" or (val_39.ClassName == "Sparkles" or (val_39.ClassName == "Fire" or (val_39.ClassName == "Smoke" or (val_39.ClassName == "Trail" or val_39.ClassName == "Beam")))))) then
            toggleParticle(
                val_39,
                v26.options.Main.AurasEnabled.Property.Value <= 2 and arg99 == LocalPlayer.Character or v26.options.Main.AurasEnabled.Property.Value == 1
            )
        end
    end
end

    end

    --  [1] v59 (ref)
    --  [2] v60 (ref)

        v59 = arg116

            false,
            Enum.KeyCode[v26.options.Controls.EMOTE.KeyString[v60]]
        )

        if not arg117 then
            newAlert("Controls set to " .. v60)
        end
    end
end

function TextChatService.OnIncomingMessage(u1: userdata) -- line: 136
    local TextChatMessageProperties = Instance.new("TextChatMessageProperties")

    if u1.TextSource then
        local v27 = game.Players:GetPlayerByUserId(u1.TextSource.UserId)

        if v27 then
            if v27:GetAttribute("ChatTag_Creator") == true then
                TextChatMessageProperties.PrefixText = "<font color='#00FF00'>[Creator] </font>" .. u1.PrefixText
                TextChatMessageProperties.Text = "<font color='#F37DC0'>" .. u1.Text .. "</font>"

                return TextChatMessageProperties
            end

            if v27:GetAttribute("ChatTag_CGTeam") == true then
                TextChatMessageProperties.PrefixText = "<font color='#0ca3f2'>[Developer] </font>" .. u1.PrefixText
                TextChatMessageProperties.Text = "<font color='#24f69b'>" .. u1.Text .. "</font>"

                return TextChatMessageProperties
            end

            if v27:GetAttribute("ChatTag_VIP") == true then
                TextChatMessageProperties.PrefixText = "<font color='#E74C3C'>[VIP] </font>" .. u1.PrefixText
                TextChatMessageProperties.Text = "<font color='#F1DB80'>" .. u1.Text .. "</font>"
            end
        end
    end

    return TextChatMessageProperties
end

function t.GetCameraMovementModeFromSettings(_) -- line: 157
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] Players (copy)
    --  [2] CameraUtils_m (copy)
    --  [3] UserInputService (copy)
    --  [4] UserGameSettings (copy)

    if Players.LocalPlayer.CameraMode == Enum.CameraMode.LockFirstPerson then
        return CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(
            Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic
        )
    end

    local v15, v16

    if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
        v15 = CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(Players.LocalPlayer.DevTouchCameraMode)
        v16 = CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(
            UserGameSettings.TouchCameraMovementMode
        )
    else
        v15 = CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(
            Players.LocalPlayer.DevComputerCameraMode
        )
        v16 = CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(
            UserGameSettings.ComputerCameraMovementMode
        )
    end

    if v15 == Enum.DevComputerCameraMovementMode.UserChoice then
        return v16
    end

    return v15
end

function t.ActivateOcclusionModule(arg4, arg5) -- line: 182
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] Poppercam_m (copy)
    --  [2] Invisicam_m (copy)
    --  [3] t_4 (copy)
    --  [4] Players (copy)

    local v17

    if arg5 == Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Zoom then
        v17 = Poppercam_m
    elseif arg5 == Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam then
        v17 = Invisicam_m
    else
        warn("CameraScript ActivateOcclusionModule called with unsupported mode")

        return
    end

    arg4.occlusionMode = arg5

    if arg4.activeOcclusionModule and arg4.activeOcclusionModule:GetOcclusionMode() == arg5 then
        if not arg4.activeOcclusionModule:GetEnabled() then
            arg4.activeOcclusionModule:Enable(true)
        end

        return
    end

    local ActiveOcclusionModule = arg4.activeOcclusionModule
    arg4.activeOcclusionModule = t_4[v17]

    if not arg4.activeOcclusionModule then
        arg4.activeOcclusionModule = v17.new()

        if arg4.activeOcclusionModule then
            t_4[v17] = arg4.activeOcclusionModule
        end
    end

    if arg4.activeOcclusionModule then
        if arg4.activeOcclusionModule:GetOcclusionMode() ~= arg5 then
            warn(
                "CameraScript ActivateOcclusionModule mismatch: ",
                arg4.activeOcclusionModule:GetOcclusionMode(),
                "~=",
                arg5
            )
        end

        if ActiveOcclusionModule then
            if ActiveOcclusionModule ~= arg4.activeOcclusionModule then
                ActiveOcclusionModule:Enable(false)
            else
                warn(
                    "CameraScript ActivateOcclusionModule failure to detect already running correct module"
                )
            end
        end

        if arg5 == Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam then
            if Players.LocalPlayer.Character then
                arg4.activeOcclusionModule:CharacterAdded(
                    Players.LocalPlayer.Character,
                    Players.LocalPlayer
                )
            end
        else
            for _, val_3 in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if val_3 and val_3.Character then
                    arg4.activeOcclusionModule:CharacterAdded(val_3.Character, val_3)
                end
            end

            arg4.activeOcclusionModule:OnCameraSubjectChanged(
                game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject
            )
        end

        arg4.activeOcclusionModule:Enable(true)
    end
end

function t.ShouldUseVehicleCamera(arg6) -- line: 261
    local CurrentCamera_1 = workspace.CurrentCamera

    if not CurrentCamera_1 then
        return false
    end

    local CameraType = CurrentCamera_1.CameraType
    local CameraSubject = CurrentCamera_1.CameraSubject
    local v19 = CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Custom or CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Follow
    local v20 = CameraSubject and CameraSubject:IsA("VehicleSeat") or false

    return v20 and (v19 and arg6.occlusionMode ~= Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam)
end

function t.ActivateCameraController(arg7, arg8, arg9) -- line: 279
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] LegacyCamera_m (copy)
    --  [2] VRService (copy)
    --  [3] VRCamera_m (copy)
    --  [4] ClassicCamera_m (copy)
    --  [5] OrbitalCamera_m (copy)
    --  [6] VRVehicleCamera_m (copy)
    --  [7] VehicleCamera_m (copy)
    --  [8] t_3 (copy)

    local v23 = nil

    if arg9 ~= nil then
        if arg9 == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
            if arg7.activeCameraController then
                arg7.activeCameraController:Enable(false)
                arg7.activeCameraController = nil
            end

            return
        end

        if arg9 == Enum.CameraType.Custom then
            arg8 = arg7:GetCameraMovementModeFromSettings()
        elseif arg9 == Enum.CameraType.Track then
            arg8 = Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic
        elseif arg9 == Enum.CameraType.Follow then
            arg8 = Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Follow
        elseif arg9 == Enum.CameraType.Orbital then
            arg8 = Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Orbital
        elseif arg9 == Enum.CameraType.Attach or (arg9 == Enum.CameraType.Watch or arg9 == Enum.CameraType.Fixed) then
            v23 = LegacyCamera_m
        else
            warn("CameraScript encountered an unhandled Camera.CameraType value: ", arg9)
        end
    end

    if not v23 then
        if VRService.VREnabled then
            v23 = VRCamera_m
        elseif arg8 == Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic or (arg8 == Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Follow or (arg8 == Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Default or arg8 == Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.CameraToggle)) then
            v23 = ClassicCamera_m
        elseif arg8 == Enum.ComputerCameraMovementMode.Orbital then
            v23 = OrbitalCamera_m
        else
            warn("ActivateCameraController did not select a module.")

            return
        end
    end

    if arg7:ShouldUseVehicleCamera() then
        v23 = if not VRService.VREnabled then VehicleCamera_m else VRVehicleCamera_m
    end

    local v24

    if not t_3[v23] then
        v24 = v23.new()
        t_3[v23] = v24
    else
        v24 = t_3[v23]

        if v24.Reset then
            v24:Reset()
        end
    end

    if arg7.activeCameraController then
        if arg7.activeCameraController ~= v24 then
            arg7.activeCameraController:Enable(false)
            arg7.activeCameraController = v24
            arg7.activeCameraController:Enable(true)
        elseif not arg7.activeCameraController:GetEnabled() then
            arg7.activeCameraController:Enable(true)
        end
    elseif v24 ~= nil then
        arg7.activeCameraController = v24
        arg7.activeCameraController:Enable(true)
    end

    if arg7.activeCameraController then
        if arg8 ~= nil then
            arg7.activeCameraController:SetCameraMovementMode(arg8)

            return
        end

        if arg9 ~= nil then
            arg7.activeCameraController:SetCameraType(arg9)
        end
    end
end

function t.OnCameraSubjectChanged(arg10) -- line: 384
    local CurrentCamera_2 = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local v25 = CurrentCamera_2 and CurrentCamera_2.CameraSubject

    if arg10.activeTransparencyController then
        arg10.activeTransparencyController:SetSubject(v25)
    end

    if arg10.activeOcclusionModule then
        arg10.activeOcclusionModule:OnCameraSubjectChanged(v25)
    end

    arg10:ActivateCameraController(nil, CurrentCamera_2.CameraType)
end

function t.OnCameraTypeChanged(arg11, arg12) -- line: 399
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] UserInputService (copy)
    --  [2] CameraUtils_m (copy)

    if arg12 == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable and UserInputService.MouseBehavior == Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter then
        CameraUtils_m.restoreMouseBehavior()
    end

    arg11:ActivateCameraController(nil, arg12)
end

function t.OnCurrentCameraChanged(arg13) -- line: 411

            -- upvalues:

function t.OnLocalPlayerCameraPropertyChanged(t1: table, s1: string) -- line: 435
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] Players (copy)
    --  [2] CameraUtils_m (copy)

    if s1 == "CameraMode" then
        if Players.LocalPlayer.CameraMode == Enum.CameraMode.LockFirstPerson then
            if t1.activeCameraController then
                if t1.activeCameraController:GetModuleName() ~= "ClassicCamera" then
                    t1:ActivateCameraController(
                        CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(
                            Enum.DevComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic
                        )
                    )
                end
            else
                t1:ActivateCameraController(
                    CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(
                        Enum.DevComputerCameraMovementMode.Classic
                    )
                )
            end

            if t1.activeCameraController then
                t1.activeCameraController:UpdateForDistancePropertyChange()

                return
            end
        else
            if Players.LocalPlayer.CameraMode == Enum.CameraMode.Classic then
                t1:ActivateCameraController(
                    CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(
                        (t1:GetCameraMovementModeFromSettings())
                    )
                )

                return
            end

            warn("Unhandled value for property player.CameraMode: ", Players.LocalPlayer.CameraMode)

            return
        end
    else
        if s1 == "DevComputerCameraMode" or s1 == "DevTouchCameraMode" then
            t1:ActivateCameraController(
                CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(
                    (t1:GetCameraMovementModeFromSettings())
                )
            )

            return
        end

        if s1 == "DevCameraOcclusionMode" then
            t1:ActivateOcclusionModule(Players.LocalPlayer.DevCameraOcclusionMode)

            return
        end

        if s1 == "CameraMinZoomDistance" or s1 == "CameraMaxZoomDistance" then
            if t1.activeCameraController then
                t1.activeCameraController:UpdateForDistancePropertyChange()

                return
            end
        else
            if s1 == "DevTouchMovementMode" then
                return
            end

            if s1 == "DevComputerMovementMode" then
                return
            end

            if s1 == "DevEnableMouseLock" then
            end
        end
    end
end

function t.GetCameraControlChoice(_) -- line: 520
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] Players (copy)
    --  [2] UserInputService (copy)
    --  [3] CameraUtils_m (copy)
    --  [4] UserGameSettings (copy)

    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

    if LocalPlayer then
        if UserInputService:GetLastInputType() == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
            if LocalPlayer.DevTouchCameraMode == Enum.DevTouchCameraMovementMode.UserChoice then
                return CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(
                    UserGameSettings.TouchCameraMovementMode
                )
            end

            return CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(LocalPlayer.DevTouchCameraMode)
        end

        if LocalPlayer.DevComputerCameraMode == Enum.DevComputerCameraMovementMode.UserChoice then
            return CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(
                (CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(
                    UserGameSettings.ComputerCameraMovementMode
                ))
            )
        end

        return CameraUtils_m.ConvertCameraModeEnumToStandard(LocalPlayer.DevComputerCameraMode)
    else
        return
    end
end

    end

function JumpPowerToHeight(arg37, arg38) -- line: 556
    local v138 = 1

    if arg38 then
        local Humanoid_1 = Instance.new("Humanoid")
        local v139 = Humanoid_1.JumpPower ^ 2 / 392.4
        local JumpHeight = Humanoid_1.JumpHeight
        Humanoid_1:Destroy()
        v138 = JumpHeight / v139
    end

    return arg37 ^ 2 / 392.4 * v138
end

function JumpHeightToPower(arg39) -- line: 569
    return (math.sqrt(2 * arg39 * workspace.Gravity))
end

    -- upvalues:

function t.calculateRawMoveVector(t2: table, u1: userdata, vec1: Vector3) -- line: 437
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] Workspace (copy)
    --  [2] VRService (copy)
    --  [3] getGamepadRightThumbstickPosition (copy)

    local CurrentCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

    if not CurrentCamera then
        return vec1
    end

    local v39 = CurrentCamera.CFrame

    if VRService.VREnabled and u1.RootPart then
        local _ = VRService:GetUserCFrame(Enum.UserCFrame.Head)
        local v41 = t2:GetEstimatedVRTorsoFrame()

        if (CurrentCamera.Focus.Position - v39.Position).Magnitude < 3 then
            v39 *= v41
        else
            v39 = CurrentCamera.CFrame * (v41.Rotation + v41.Position * CurrentCamera.HeadScale)
        end
    end

    if u1:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then
        if VRService.VREnabled then
            vec1 = Vector3.new(vec1.X, 0, vec1.Z)

            if vec1.Magnitude < 0.01 then
                return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end

            local v42 = -getGamepadRightThumbstickPosition().Y * 1.3962634015954636
            local v43 = math.atan2(-vec1.X, -vec1.Z)
            local _, v45, _ = v39:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()

            return CFrame.fromEulerAnglesYXZ(v42, v43 + v45, 0).LookVector
        else
            return v39:VectorToWorldSpace(vec1)
        end
    else
        local _, _, _, v50, v51, v52, _, _, v55, _, _, v58 = v39:GetComponents()
        local v59, v60

        if v55 < 1 and v55 > -1 then
            v59 = v58
            v60 = v52
        else
            v59 = v50
            v60 = -v51 * math.sign(v55)
        end

        local v62 = math.sqrt(v59 * v59 + v60 * v60)

        return (Vector3.new(
            (v59 * vec1.X + v60 * vec1.Z) / v62,
            0,
            (v59 * vec1.Z - v60 * vec1.X) / v62
        ))
    end
end

    --  [2] v7 (ref)

function t.OnLastInputTypeChanged(arg29, arg30) -- line: 669
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] v12 (ref)
    --  [2] t_2 (copy)

    if v12 == arg30 then
        warn("LastInputType Change listener called with current type.")
    end

    v12 = arg30

    if v12 == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        local v77, v78 = arg29:SelectTouchModule()

        if v78 then

                if arg29.touchControlFrame then

            arg29:SwitchToController(v77)
        end
    elseif t_2[v12] ~= nil then
        local v79 = arg29:SelectComputerMovementModule()

        if v79 then
            arg29:SwitchToController(v79)
        end
    end

local function thumbstickCurve(arg5) -- line: 76
    local v8 = (math.exp((math.abs(arg5) - 0.1) / 0.9 * 2) - 1) / 6.38905609893065

local function adjustTouchPitchSensitivity(u1: Vector2) -- line: 90
    local CurrentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

    if not CurrentCamera then
        return u1
    end

    local v10 = CurrentCamera.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()

    if u1.Y * v10 >= 0 then
        return u1
    end

    return Vector2.new(1, (1 - (math.abs(v10) * 2 / 3.141592653589793) ^ 0.75) * 0.75 + 0.25) * u1
end

local function IsValidParticle(u1: userdata) -- line: 40
    if not u1:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
        if not u1:IsA("Beam") then
            if not u1:IsA("Trail") then
                if not u1:IsA("Fire") then
                    if not u1:IsA("Smoke") then
                        if u1:IsA("Sparkles") then
                            return true
                        end

                        return false
                    else
                        return true
                    end
                else
                    return true
                end
            else
                return true
            end
        else
            return true
        end
    else
        return true
    end
end

local function SearchCharacter() -- line: 54
    -- upvalues:

    --  [2] IsValidParticle (copy)

        if IsValidParticle(descendant) and typeof(t[descendant]) ~= "boolean" then
            t[descendant] = descendant.Enabled
        end
    end
end

    end

        end

            end

                        key_16

                    end
                end

                if v65.MapStats.TotalMaps:FindFirstChild(key_16) then
                    v65.MapStats.TotalMaps:FindFirstChild(key_16).Text = v68
                end
            end

            if key_16 == "mapSurvivalRatio" then
                v68 = {}

                for index_3 = 1, 6 do

                        v70 = v1.gameplayStats.mapsPlayed[index_3] < 100 and v1.gameplayStats.mapsSurvived[index_3] or val_16[index_3]
                        local v71 = math.clamp(v1.gameplayStats.mapsPlayed[index_3], 0, 100)

                            key_16

                local v74 = 0

                    for index_4 = 1, #v68 do

                            v73 += v68[index_4].Played

function GetAABB(u1: userdata) -- line: 148
    local math_abs: any = math.abs
    local Size: any = u1.Size
    local X: any = Size.X
    local Y: any = Size.Y
    local Z: any = Size.Z
    local _: any, _, _, v7, v8, v9, v10, v11, v12, v13, v14, v15 = u1.CFrame:GetComponents()
    local v18: any = math_abs(v7) * X + math_abs(v8) * Y + math_abs(v9) * Z
    local v21: any = math_abs(v10) * X + math_abs(v11) * Y + math_abs(v12) * Z
    local v23 = math_abs(v13) * X + math_abs(v14) * Y

    return (Vector3.new(v18, v21, v23 + math_abs(v15) * Z))
end

        overlapParams.RespectCanCollide = false
        overlapParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = t3
        overlapParams.MaxParts = 100

                t4[val_5] = true

            end
        end

        return t4
    end

        if not (#t_5 > 0) and not (#t_4 > 0) then
            break

    ["Enum.NormalId.Back"] = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)

local function GetValueFromNumberSequence(n1: number, u1: userdata) -- line: 141
    for index = 1, #u1.Keypoints - 1 do
        if n1 >= u1.Keypoints[index].Time and n1 <= u1.Keypoints[index + 1].Time then
            local v6 = (n1 - u1.Keypoints[index].Time) / (u1.Keypoints[index + 1].Time - u1.Keypoints[index].Time)

            return (1 - v6) * u1.Keypoints[index].Value + v6 * u1.Keypoints[index + 1].Value
        end
    end

    return 0
end

local function GetValueFromColorSequence(n2: number, u2: userdata) -- line: 153
    local v7 = nil

    for index_1 = 1, #u2.Keypoints - 1 do
        local _ = #u2.Keypoints - 1

        if n2 >= u2.Keypoints[index_1].Time and n2 <= u2.Keypoints[index_1 + 1].Time then
            v7 = u2.Keypoints[index_1].Value:Lerp(
                u2.Keypoints[index_1 + 1].Value,
                (n2 - u2.Keypoints[index_1].Time) / (u2.Keypoints[index_1 + 1].Time - u2.Keypoints[index_1].Time)
            )
        end
    end

    return v7
end

local _ = function(u3: userdata) --[[ GenerateNumberSequenceFromEnvelope ]] -- line: 165
    local t_6 = {}

    for index_2 = 1, #u3.Keypoints do
        local v9 = u3.Keypoints[index_2].Envelope * 1000
        local v10 = u3.Keypoints[index_2].Value * 1000 - v9
        local _ = #u3.Keypoints
        table.insert(
            t_6,
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(
                u3.Keypoints[index_2].Time,
                v10 + math.random(0, v9 * 2) / 1000
            )
        )
    end

    return t_6
end

local _ = function(u4: NumberRange) --[[ GetValueFromNumberRange ]] -- line: 176
    return math.random(u4.Min * 100, u4.Max * 100) * 0.01
end

local function SimulateParticle(t1: table, n3: number, n4: number?) -- line: 180
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] GetValueFromNumberSequence (copy)
    --  [2] GetValueFromColorSequence (copy)

    local v12 = n3 * t1.FakeParticleProps.TimeScale
    local v13 = n4 or v12 / t1.FakeParticleProps.Lifetime

    if v13 >= 1 then
        t1.FakeParticle:Destroy()

        return
    end

    if not t1.FakeParticle:FindFirstChild("ParticleFront") then
        return
    end

    local v14 = GetValueFromNumberSequence(v13, t1.FakeParticleProps.Size) * 1.8
    t1.FakeParticle.Size = Vector3.new(
        t1.FakeParticleProps.Texture_Ratio.X * v14,
        t1.FakeParticleProps.Texture_Ratio.Y * v14,
        1
    )
    local v15 = GetValueFromColorSequence(v13, t1.FakeParticleProps.Color)
    local v16 = GetValueFromNumberSequence(v13, t1.FakeParticleProps.Transparency)
    t1.FakeParticle.ParticleFront.Color3 = v15
    t1.FakeParticle.ParticleFront.Transparency = v16
    t1.FakeParticle.ParticleBack.Color3 = v15
    t1.FakeParticle.ParticleBack.Transparency = v16
    t1.ParticleRot += t1.FakeParticleProps.RotSpeed * v12
    t1.ParticleVel = (t1.ParticleVel + t1.FakeParticleProps.Acceleration * v12) * 2 ^ (v12 * -t1.FakeParticleProps.Drag)
    t1.ParticlePos += t1.ParticleVel * v12
    local v17 = nil
    v17 = if t1.FakeParticleProps.Orientation == Enum.ParticleOrientation.FacingCamera then -t1.ViewportFrame.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector else if t1.FakeParticleProps.Orientation == Enum.ParticleOrientation.FacingCameraWorldUp then -Vector3.new(
        t1.ViewportFrame.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector.X,
        0,
        t1.ViewportFrame.CurrentCamera.CFrame.LookVector.Z
    ) else if t1.FakeParticleProps.Orientation == Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityPerpendicular then t1.ParticleVel else if t1.FakeParticleProps.Orientation ~= Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel then v17 else t1.ParticleVel
    local v18 = if t1.FakeParticleProps.LockedToPart ~= true then t1.ParticlePos else (t1.OriginalParticle.Parent.CFrame * CFrame.new(
        t1.ParticlePos.X,
        t1.ParticlePos.Y,
        t1.ParticlePos.Z
    )).Position or t1.ParticlePos
    t1.FakeParticle.CFrame = CFrame.new(v18, v18 + v17) * CFrame.Angles(
        0,
        0,
        (math.rad(t1.ParticleRot))
    )
end

local function CreateParticle(u5: userdata, u6: userdata, n5: number?) -- line: 221

function t.InitParticle(u8: userdata, b1: boolean, u9: userdata) -- line: 374

    --  [1] CreateParticle (copy)

    local v40 = u8:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("ViewportFrame")

    if b1 == true then
        local v41 = 1 / (u8.Rate * u8.TimeScale)

        for index_4 = 0, u8.Lifetime.Max, v41 do
            local _ = u8.Lifetime.Max
            CreateParticle(u8, v40, index_4)
        end
    else
        local function StartAuraViewUpdate() -- line: 381
            -- upvalues:
            --  [1] u8 (copy)
            --  [2] u9 (copy)
            --  [3] CreateParticle (ref)
            --  [4] v40 (copy)

            while true do
                if not u8 then
                    break
                end

                if not task.wait(1 / (u8.Rate * u8.TimeScale)) or (not u9.Visible or not u8.Parent) then
                    break
                end

                if not u8.Enabled then
                    continue
                end

                if not u8.Parent then
                    continue
                end

                CreateParticle(u8, v40)
            end
        end

        local _ = nil

        local function OnVisiblityChanged() -- line: 393
            -- upvalues:
            --  [1] u9 (copy)
            --  [2] StartAuraViewUpdate (copy)

            if u9.Visible then
                StartAuraViewUpdate()
            end
        end

        local v43 = u9:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(OnVisiblityChanged)
        task.defer(OnVisiblityChanged)
        u8.Destroying:Once(function() -- line: 404
            -- upvalues:
            --  [1] v43 (ref)

            if v43 then
                v43:Disconnect()
            end
        end)
    end
end

    end
end

function t_1._StepRotation(arg8, arg9, arg10) -- line: 85
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] CameraInput_m (copy)
    --  [2] SanitizeAngle (copy)
    --  [3] VehicleCameraConfig_m (copy)
    --  [4] MapClamp (copy)

    local YawSpring = arg8.yawSpring
    local PitchSpring = arg8.pitchSpring
    local v15 = CameraInput_m.getRotation(true)
    local v16 = -v15.Y
    YawSpring.pos = SanitizeAngle(YawSpring.pos + -v15.X)
    PitchSpring.pos = SanitizeAngle(
        (math.clamp(PitchSpring.pos + v16, -1.3962634015954636, 1.3962634015954636))
    )

    if CameraInput_m.getRotationActivated() then
        arg8.lastPanTick = os.clock()
    end

    local v18 = -math.rad(VehicleCameraConfig_m.pitchBaseAngle)
    local v19 = math.rad(VehicleCameraConfig_m.pitchDeadzoneAngle)

    if os.clock() - arg8.lastPanTick > VehicleCameraConfig_m.autocorrectDelay then
        local v20 = MapClamp(
            arg10,
            VehicleCameraConfig_m.autocorrectMinCarSpeed,
            VehicleCameraConfig_m.autocorrectMaxCarSpeed,
            0,
            VehicleCameraConfig_m.autocorrectResponse
        )
        YawSpring.freq = v20
        PitchSpring.freq = v20

        if YawSpring.freq < 0.001 then
            YawSpring.vel = 0
        end

        if PitchSpring.freq < 0.001 then
            PitchSpring.vel = 0
        end

        if v19 >= math.abs((SanitizeAngle(v18 - PitchSpring.pos))) then
            PitchSpring.goal = PitchSpring.pos
        else
            PitchSpring.goal = v18
        end
    else
        YawSpring.freq = 0
        YawSpring.vel = 0
        PitchSpring.freq = 0
        PitchSpring.vel = 0
        PitchSpring.goal = v18
    end

    return CFrame.fromEulerAnglesYXZ(PitchSpring:step(arg9), YawSpring:step(arg9), 0)
end

end

function t.Step(t1: table, n5: number) -- line: 49
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] math_exp (copy)

    local v4 = t1.freq * 2 * 3.141592653589793
    local V = t1.v
    local MinValue = t1.minValue
    local MaxValue = t1.maxValue
    local Goal = t1.goal
    local v5 = Goal - t1.x
    local v6 = v4 * n5
    local v7 = math_exp(-v6)
    local v8 = Goal + (V * n5 - v5 * (v6 + 1)) * v7
    local v9 = ((v5 * v4 - V) * v6 + V) * v7

    if MinValue > v8 then
        v8 = MinValue
        v9 = 0
    elseif MaxValue < v8 then
        v8 = MaxValue
        v9 = 0
    end

    t1.x = v8
    t1.v = v9

    return v8
end

return {
    CanUseDevTools = function(arg1, arg2) --[[ CanUseDevTools ]] -- line: 10
        -- upvalues:
        --  [1] FE2Library_m (copy)

        if not arg1 then
            return
        end

        if typeof(arg1) ~= "Instance" then
            return
        end

        if not arg2 then
            return
        end

        local v1 = arg2

        if typeof(v1) == "Instance" then
            v1 = { v1 }
        end

        if typeof(v1) ~= "table" then
            return
        end

        local t_1 = {}

        if FE2Library_m.DEBUG_FLAGS.DEV_TOOLS_ENABLED then
            for _, val in v1 do
                table.insert(t_1, val)
            end
        end

        if arg1:FindFirstChild("Settings") then
            local v4 = (arg1.Settings:GetAttribute("AuthorId") or "") .. "," .. (arg1.Settings:GetAttribute(
                "Collaborators"
            ) or "") .. "," .. (arg1.Settings:GetAttribute("Testers") or "") .. ",247535,4182655268"

            for _, val_1 in next, v1 do
                if not string.find(v4, val_1.UserId) then
                    if arg1.Settings:GetAttribute("AllowAllToTest") == true then
                        table.insert(t_1, val_1)
                    end
                else
                    table.insert(t_1, val_1)
                end
            end
        end

        return t_1
    end
}

function t_1.isGrounded(arg1) -- line: 132
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] t (copy)

    local v3 = arg1.rootPart.CFrame
    local v4 = 0.4 * arg1.rootPart.Size
    local Y = arg1.rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y
    local v5 = 1.5
    local v6 = -125 * v4.Y

    if Y > 0 then
        return false
    end

    if Y < v6 then
        v5 += Y / v6
    end

    local v7 = 0.6 * v4.Y + arg1.humanoid.HipHeight
    local v8

    if arg1.isRigTypeR6 then
        v8 = 0
        local LeftLeg = arg1.character:FindFirstChild("Left Leg")
        local RightLeg = arg1.character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")

        if LeftLeg and LeftLeg:IsA("BasePart") then
            v8 = math.max(v8, LeftLeg.Size.Y)
        end

        if RightLeg and RightLeg:IsA("BasePart") then
            v8 = math.max(v8, RightLeg.Size.Y)
        end

        v7 += v8
    end

    v8 = v7 * v5 * Vector3.new(-0, -1, -0)
    arg1:resetLines()

    for _, val in t do
        local v10 = v3:PointToWorldSpace(v4 * val)
        local v11 = workspace:Raycast(v10, v8, arg1.params)
        arg1:drawLine(v10, v8, v11)

        if v11 then
            return true
        end
    end

    return false
end

        arg2.humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed)
    end

    debug.profileend()
end

function t.UseViewport(t2: table) -- line: 505
    return if t2.UseThumbnail == true then false else t2.catagory == "Emotes" or (t2.catagory == "Animations" or (t2.catagory == "Tanks" or (t2.catagory == "Skins" or (t2.catagory == "Buddies" or t2.catagory == "Auras"))))
end

function t.DisplayItemImgOrViewport(
    t3: table,
    u8: userdata,
    u9: userdata,
    b1: boolean,
    n1: number,
    u10: userdata
) -- line: 509
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] t (copy)

    local v90 = t.UseViewport(t3)
    u8.Visible = not v90
    u9.Visible = v90

    if v90 then
        task.delay((n1 or 0) / 60, function() -- line: 515
            -- upvalues:
            --  [1] u9 (copy)
            --  [2] t (ref)
            --  [3] t3 (copy)
            --  [4] u10 (copy)

            if u9.Parent then
                t.ConfigureViewport(u9, t3, u10)
            end
        end)

        return
    end

    local v91 = t3.imgID
    v91 = b1 and t3.featuredImgID or v91
    u8.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. v91
end

return t

function t.InitBadgeInfo(arg2) -- line: 943
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] t (copy)
    --  [2] BadgeService (copy)
    --  [3] v1 (copy)

    task.spawn(function() -- line: 944
        -- upvalues:
        --  [1] t (ref)
        --  [2] BadgeService (ref)
        --  [3] v1 (ref)
        --  [4] arg2 (copy)

        local v2

        for _, val_1 in next, t.badgeIds do
            for _, val_2 in next, t.BadgeData do
                if val_2.BadgeId == val_1 then
                    v2 = val_2
                end
            end

            v2 = nil

            if not v2 then
                local _ = {}
                v2 = BadgeService:GetBadgeInfoAsync(val_1)
                v2.BadgeId = val_1
                table.insert(t.BadgeData, v2)
            end
        end

        if not v1 then
            t.UpdateOwnershipCache(true)
        end

        t.LoadedBadges = true

        if arg2 == true then
            print(t.BadgeData)
        end
    end)
end

function t.GetInfoFromId(arg3) -- line: 970
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] t (copy)
    --  [2] BadgeService (copy)

    if not arg3 then
        return
    end

    if t.LoadedBadges ~= true then
        warn("Badge data is still loading, call once done")

        return
    end

    local v4

    for _, val_3 in next, t.BadgeData do
        if val_3.BadgeId == arg3 then
            v4 = val_3
        end
    end

    v4 = nil

    if v4 then
        return v4
    end

    local v5 = nil

    if pcall(function() -- line: 985
        -- upvalues:
        --  [1] v5 (ref)
        --  [2] BadgeService (ref)
        --  [3] arg3 (copy)

        v5 = BadgeService:GetBadgeInfoAsync(arg3)
    end) and v5 then
        v5.BadgeId = arg3
        local v6

        for _, val_4 in next, t.BadgeData do
            if val_4.BadgeId == arg3 then
                v6 = val_4
            end
        end

        v6 = nil

        if not v6 then
            table.insert(t.BadgeData, v5)

            for _, val_5 in next, t.BadgeData do
                if val_5.BadgeId == arg3 then
                    v6 = val_5
                end
            end

            v6 = nil

            if v6 then
                return v6
            end
        end
    else
        warn("Badge not found")
    end
end

function t.UpdateOwnershipCache(arg4) -- line: 1000
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] v1 (copy)
    --  [2] now (ref)
    --  [3] t (copy)
    --  [4] BadgeService (copy)

    print("Updating badge ownership...")

    if v1 then
        warn("This function can only be used on the client")

        return
    end

    if arg4 ~= true and tick() - 60 < now then
        return
    end

    now = tick()
    local v7 = 1
    local Result
    local v8

    while true do
        if not (v7 <= #t.BadgeData) then
            break
        end

        local t_115 = {}

        repeat
            if t.BadgeData[v7].OwnsBadge ~= true then
                table.insert(t_115, t.BadgeData[v7].BadgeId)
            end

            v7 += 1
        until #t_115 >= 10 or t.BadgeData[v7] == nil

        repeat
            local Success_1
            Success_1, Result = pcall(
                BadgeService.CheckUserBadgesAsync,
                BadgeService,
                game.Players.LocalPlayer.UserId,
                t_115
            )
            v8 = Success_1

            if not v8 then
                print("BadgeInfo: Rate limited, yielding")
                task.wait(30)
            end
        until v8

        for _, val_6 in Result do
            for _, val_7 in t.BadgeData do
                if val_7.BadgeId == val_6 then
                    val_7.OwnsBadge = true
                end
            end
        end
    end

    print("Badge Ownership Refreshed")
end

t.InitBadgeInfo()

return t

local t = {}
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local APIService_m = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Shared.APIService)

function t.SetHumanoid(arg1, arg2, arg3, arg4) -- line: 10
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] RunService (copy)

    local t_1 = { "Jump", "Health", "MaxSlopeAngle", "WalkSpeed", "JumpPower" }

    for _, val in next, arg2 do
        if val.Character and val.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
            for key_1, val_1 in pairs(arg3) do
                if table.find(t_1, key_1) then
                    if key_1 == "WalkSpeed" then
                        val.Character.Humanoid[key_1] = val_1 * arg4
                    else
                        val.Character.Humanoid[key_1] = val_1
                    end

                    if key_1 == "Jump" then
                        val.Character.Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if RunService:IsClient() then
        game.ReplicatedStorage.Remote.ClientTimelines.ValidateLocalSetHumanoid:FireServer(arg1)
    end
end

local now = tick()

function t.ShakeCamera(arg5, arg6) -- line: 40
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] RunService (copy)
    --  [2] now (ref)

    if not RunService:IsClient() then
        return
    end

    now = tick()
    local Humanoid = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

    if not Humanoid then
        return
    end

    local v1 = math.floor(arg5 * 20)
    local v2 = tick() + arg6
    task.spawn(function() -- line: 47
        -- upvalues:
        --  [1] now (ref)
        --  [2] RunService (ref)
        --  [3] Humanoid (copy)
        --  [4] v2 (copy)
        --  [5] v1 (copy)

        local v3 = now

        while true do
            if not RunService.Heartbeat:wait() then
                break
            end

            if not (Humanoid.Health > 0) or v3 ~= now then
                break
            end

            local v5 = v2 - tick()

            if v5 <= 0 then
                return
            end

            local v6 = math.random(-v1, v1) * 0.05 * v5
            local vec3 = Vector3.new(
                v6,
                math.random(-v1, v1) * 0.05 * v5,
                math.random(-v1, v1) * 0.05 * v5
            )
            Humanoid.CameraOffset = vec3
            Humanoid:SetAttribute("CamShakeOffset", vec3)
        end
    end)

function t.PerformClientXFrame(_, arg32, arg33) -- line: 226
    -- upvalues:
    --  [1] RunService (copy)
    --  [2] t (copy)

    local v33 = arg33 or 1
    local v34 = arg32

    end

    if XFrameFunction then
        if typeof(XFrameFunction) ~= "string" then

        end
    else
        error("Client XFrame contains invalid function")
    end

    if XFrameTimestamp then
        if typeof(XFrameTimestamp) ~= "number" then
            error("Client XFrame contains invalid Timestamp")
        end
    else
        error("Client XFrame contains invalid Timestamp")
    end

    if v35 then
        if typeof(v35) ~= "number" then
            error("Client XFrame contains invalid Length")
        end
    else
        error("Client XFrame contains invalid Length")
    end

            local t_5 = {}

            for key_13, val_13 in pairs(v34.Attributes) do
                if string.find(key_13, "Property_") then
                    t_5[string.sub(key_13, 10)] = val_13
                end

            SetProperties(
                v34.Value,
                t_5,
                v34.Attributes.XFrame_ApplyToDescendants or v34.Attributes.ApplyToDescendants
            )
        end

    end

            if string.find(key_14, "Property_") then

            end

            "LocalMapCamera"
        )

            LocalMapCamera,
            Value_1,
            Enabled,
            CamInfo,

            v34.Attributes.DisableOnEnd and v35 or nil

    end

        return

        t.Teleport(v34.Value, game.Players.LocalPlayer, v34.Attributes.TranslateDestination)

        return
    end

        t.Alert(v34.Attributes.Message, v34.Attributes.Color, v35)

        return
    end

        t.Sound(
            v34.Value,
            v34.Attributes.SoundId,
            v34.Attributes.Volume or 1,
            (v34.Attributes.Pitch or 1) * v33
